# FLIXO Error Teaching Corpus — Expanded Chapter D

> Rules 4001–5000 are deterministic diagnostic teaching cases. They guide investigation; current exact-SHA evidence remains authoritative.

T4001 | class=stale-sha | stage=observe | trigger=Case 4001: stale-sha appears at the observe boundary while capture the exact failure boundary before naming a root cause. | invariant=stale-sha must remain causally identifiable, bounded, and verifiable at the exact execution boundary. | action=For case 4001, inspect stale-sha evidence first; capture the exact failure boundary before naming a root cause; do not promote a hypothesis to source mutation without fresh proof. | teaching=Capture the exact failure boundary before naming a root cause. Keep the diagnostic hypothesis separate from the observable symptom and preserve fail-closed behavior. | verify=the diagnostic record binds evidence, root cause, repair and verification to one exact SHA
T4002 | class=concurrency | stage=detect | trigger=Case 4002: concurrency appears at the detect boundary while compare current state with the failure evidence on the same exact SHA. | invariant=concurrency must remain causally identifiable, bounded, and verifiable at the exact execution boundary. | action=For case 4002, inspect concurrency evidence first; compare current state with the failure evidence on the same exact SHA; do not promote a hypothesis to source mutation without fresh proof. | teaching=Compare current state with the failure evidence on the same exact SHA. Keep the diagnostic hypothesis separate from the observable symptom and preserve fail-closed behavior. | verify=the claimed root cause remains reproducible until the correction is applied
